podgreatpersons.0001 = {
	type = character_event
	title = podgreatpersons.0001.t
	desc = podgreatpersons.0001.desc
	theme = greatperson 
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:gp_ald
		animation = personality_rational
		}
	
	trigger = { 
		}
	
	immediate =	
	{		
		create_character = {
			name = "Abd_al_Latif_al_Baghdadi" 
			age = 69 
			gender = male
			dynasty = none
			learning = 5
			diplomacy = 5
			intrigue = 5
			stewardship = 5
			faith = faith:innocence
			culture = culture:bedouin
			trait = education_learning_4
			trait = intellect_good_1
			trait = diligent
			trait = fickle
			trait = gregarious
			trait = scholar
			trait = theologian
			trait = physician_3
			trait = mortal
			random_traits = no		
			location = root.capital_province	
			save_scope_as = gp_ald
			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_the_physician
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_ald = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_ald
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_ald 
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.a	
		
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_ald
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_ald = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}	
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}			
	}	
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.b
		
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
	
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom	
	
		hidden_effect = {
	
		add_courtier = scope:gp_ald
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_ald = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}	

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.c
		
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.customb
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		scope:gp_ald = {
			move_to_pool = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.d
		
		trigger = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_ald		
		
		root = {
			save_scope_as = vampiresire
			}	
		scope:gp_ald = {
			save_scope_as = created_vampirechilde
			}
		scope:vampiresire = {
			POD_player_character_create_vampirechilde_effect = yes			
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}	
}

		character = scope:gp_geyer
		animation = personality_callous
		}
	
	trigger = { 
		}
	
	immediate =	
	{		
		create_character = {
			name = "Florian_Geyer" 
			age = 35
			gender = male
			dynasty = none
			martial = 12
			stewardship = 10
			intrigue = 10
			learning = 10
			prowess = 20
			faith = faith:innocence
			culture = culture:franconian
			trait = education_martial_prowess_3
			trait = callous
			trait = vengeful
			trait = wrathful
			trait = mortal
			random_traits = no		
			location = root.capital_province	
			save_scope_as = gp_geyer
			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_black_company
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_geyer = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_geyer
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_geyer
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.a	
		
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_geyer
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_geyer = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}	
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}			
	}	
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.b
		
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
	
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom	
	
		hidden_effect = {
	
		add_courtier = scope:gp_geyer
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_geyer = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}	

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.c
		
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.customb
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		scope:gp_geyer = {
			move_to_pool = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.d
		
		trigger = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_geyer		
		
		root = {
			save_scope_as = vampiresire
			}	
		scope:gp_geyer = {
			save_scope_as = created_vampirechilde
			}
		scope:vampiresire = {
			POD_player_character_create_vampirechilde_effect = yes			
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}	
}

		}

		}

		create_character = {
			name = "Hermann_von_Salza" 
			age = 72
			gender = male
			dynasty = none
			martial = 10
			diplomacy = 7
			learning = 10
			stewardship = 15
			faith = faith:innocence
			culture = culture:saxon

			trait = patient
			trait = gregarious
			trait = diligent
			trait = strategist
			trait = mortal
			trait = diplomat
			random_traits = no		
			location = root.capital_province	
			save_scope_as = gp_hermann
			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_spurned_grandmaster
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_hermann = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_hermann
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_hermann
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}

	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.a

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {

				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving

					}
				}	
			}

		}			
	}

						years = 2

				}
		}
		ai_chance = {

	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:gp_pons
		animation = flirtation
		}
	
	trigger = { 
		}
	
	immediate =	
	{		
		create_character = {
			name = "Pons_de_Capduelh" 
			age = 46
			gender = male
			dynasty = none
			diplomacy = 10
			learning = 10
			intrigue = 10
			faith = faith:innocence
			culture = culture:occitan
			trait = education_diplomacy_3
			trait = content
			trait = stubborn
			trait = gregarious
			trait = gallant
			trait = mortal
			random_traits = no		
			location = root.capital_province	
			save_scope_as = gp_pons
			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_popular_troubadour
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_pons = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_pons
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_pons
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.a	
		
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_pons
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_pons = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}	
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}			
	}	
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.b
		
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
	
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom	
	
		hidden_effect = {
	
		add_courtier = scope:gp_pons
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_pons = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}	

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.c
		
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.customb
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		scope:gp_pons = {
			move_to_pool = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.d
		
		trigger = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_pons		
		
		root = {
			save_scope_as = vampiresire
			}	
		scope:gp_pons = {
			save_scope_as = created_vampirechilde
			}
		scope:vampiresire = {
			POD_player_character_create_vampirechilde_effect = yes			
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}	
}

	{		
		create_character = {
			name = "Tomas_de_Torquemada" 
			age = 71
			gender = male
			dynasty = none
			intrigue = 8
			learning = 10

			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_hammer_heretics
			}
		}

				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_torquemada
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_torquemada
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"

		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_ontsifor
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_ontsifor = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}		
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}			
	}

		}

			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0

			is_ai = yes
		}
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_ontsifor		
		
		root = {
			save_scope_as = vampiresire

podgreatpersons.0093 = {
	type = character_event
	title = podgreatpersons.0093.t
	desc = podgreatpersons.0093.desc
	theme = greatperson 
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:gp_ivan
		animation = personality_content
		}
	
	trigger = { 
		}
	
	immediate =	
	{		
		create_character = {
			name = "Ivan" 
			age = 25
			gender = male
			dynasty = none
			martial = 5
			learning = 0
			intrigue = 5
			stewardship = 10
			diplomacy = 12
			prowess = 10
			faith = faith:innocence
			culture = culture:russian
			trait = education_diplomacy_3
			trait = intellect_bad_1
			trait = brave
			trait = compassionate
			trait = content
			trait = mortal
			random_traits = no		
			location = root.capital_province	
			save_scope_as = gp_ivan
			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_the_ninny
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_ivan = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_ivan
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_ivan
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}

			diplomacy = 5
			prowess = 15
			faith = faith:innocence
			culture = culture:serbian
			trait = education_martial_prowess_4
			trait = diligent
			trait = deceitful
			trait = brave
			trait = blademaster_2
			trait = mortal
			random_traits = no		
			location = root.capital_province	
			save_scope_as = gp_milos
			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_slayer_of_sultans
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_milos = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_milos
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_milos
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.a	
		
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_milos
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_milos = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}		
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}			
	}	
	
	option =

		hidden_effect = {

						years = 2
					}
				}
		}
		ai_chance = {

podgreatpersons.0106 = {
	type = character_event
	title = podgreatpersons.0106.t
	desc = podgreatpersons.0106.desc
	theme = greatperson 
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:gp_vyrodkov
		animation = personality_rational
		}
	
	trigger = { 
		}
	
	immediate =	
	{		
		create_character = {
			name = "Ivan_Vyrodkov" 
			age = 48
			gender = male
			dynasty = none
			martial = 8
			learning = 15
			intrigue = 7
			stewardship = 8
			diplomacy = 4
			prowess = 10
			faith = faith:innocence
			culture = culture:russian
			trait = education_martial_3
			trait = brave
			trait = patient
			trait = diligent
			trait = military_engineer
			trait = architect
			trait = mortal
			random_traits = no		
			location = root.capital_province	
			save_scope_as = gp_vyrodkov
			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_architect_of_war
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_vyrodkov = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_vyrodkov
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_vyrodkov
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.a	
		
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_vyrodkov
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_vyrodkov = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}		
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}			
	}	
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.b
		
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
	
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom	
	
		hidden_effect = {
	
		add_courtier = scope:gp_vyrodkov
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_vyrodkov = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}	

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.c
		
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.customb
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		scope:gp_vyrodkov = {
			move_to_pool = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}

	option =

		name = podgreatpersons.0001.d

			is_ai = yes
		}

			trait = intellect_good_1
			trait = deceitful
			trait = content
			trait = diligent

			location = root.capital_province

			give_nickname = nick_chief_minister
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_madan = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_madan
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_madan
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.a	
		
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_madan
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_madan = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}		
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}			
	}	
	
	option =

					}
				}
		}

		character = scope:gp_cabral
		animation = personality_bold
		}
	
	trigger = { 
		}
	
	immediate =	
	{		
		create_character = {
			name = "Pedro_Alvares_Cabral" 
			age = 52
			gender = male
			dynasty = none
			martial = 17
			learning = 8
			intrigue = 5
			stewardship = 9
			diplomacy = 7
			prowess = 5
			faith = faith:innocence
			culture = culture:portuguese
			trait = education_stewardship_3	
			trait = gregarious
			trait = diligent
			trait = callous
			trait = adventurer
			trait = mortal
			random_traits = no		
			location = root.capital_province	
			save_scope_as = gp_cabral
			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_spurned_explorer
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_cabral = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_cabral
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_cabral
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.a	
		
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_cabral
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_cabral = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}		
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}			
	}	
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.b
		
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
	
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom	
	
		hidden_effect = {
	
		add_courtier = scope:gp_cabral
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_cabral = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}	

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.c
		
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.customb
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		scope:gp_cabral = {
			move_to_pool = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.d
		
		trigger = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_cabral		
		
		root = {
			save_scope_as = vampiresire
			}	
		scope:gp_cabral = {
			save_scope_as = created_vampirechilde
			}
		scope:vampiresire = {
			POD_player_character_create_vampirechilde_effect = yes			
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}	
}
